Invoke to CHECK a finished blog/article draft against the SEO Playbook pre-publish checklist before it reaches Reyhan — review only, never writing. Returns a pass/fail per item and an overall 100%-or-list-the-fails verdict, counting real occurrences (not estimates). Part of the two-gate QA required on every article (the other gate is contentos-gate-editorial). Do NOT invoke for drafting, research, or non-review tasks. - **ContestEditor**: Polishes a written contest deliverable against each `.harness/gate.json` criterion — referents, sentence length, term consistency, the tone the notice asks for, and length limits — editing the files in place without changing content, numbers, or claims, and returns the list of edits and a per-criterion style judgment. - **ContestGate**: Reads one contest's official notice and attachments (PDF/HWP), captures their actual bytes as immutable rule snapshots and records a provenance-backed structured rule review (`ach rules capture|review`), checks eligibility against `ach profile eligibility --json` (no identifying values), fills the CONTRACT §7 gate.json exactly (type, level 1-5, priority, criteria, deliverables), and records it with `ach gate &lt;id&gt; --file &lt;path&gt;`. Marks already-submitted contests (submission ledger) as duplicate. At most 3 questions for the owner. ([FMsongX2/Autocontest-Harness](https://github.com/FMsongX2/Autocontest-Harness/blob/HEAD/agents/ContestGate.md))
- **ContestHQ**: Runs a level 3-5 contest end to end inside its folder: writes RUBRIC/SPEC/PLAN/TASKS from the harness templates, runs the score loop (ContestJudge predicts plan scores for 2-3 candidates and the plan is improved before building, then draft and final scores, each recorded with `ach score`), gathers evidence with DomainResearcher/ArticleResearcher, decides what to reuse instead of build with OpenSourceScout whenever the deliverable includes code, a model, a service, an experiment pipeline, or data tooling (and production references with ReferenceScout at level 5), dispatches worker waves sized… ([FMsongX2/Autocontest-Harness](https://github.com/FMsongX2/Autocontest-Harness/blob/HEAD/agents/ContestHQ.md))
- **ContestJudge**: Strict contest reviewer. Stage `plan` predicts the score a concept/SPEC/PLAN would earn if completed as planned, judged against this contest's organizer/host, purpose and judging criteria (reported as `basis`), and proposes per-criterion plan changes that raise it; stages `draft` and `final` run gated compliance checks (format, limits, eligibility, AI disclosure, rule violations) first, map every notice rule and judging criterion to where the deliverable satisfies it, then score each `.harness/gate.json` criterion. Returns stage, total, target, verdict (ready | revise | reject), per-criterion… ([FMsongX2/Autocontest-Harness](https://github.com/FMsongX2/Autocontest-Harness/blob/HEAD/agents/ContestJudge.md))
- **ContestScout**: Finds new contests that the 11 `ach collect` sources (linkareer, wevity, thinkcontest, allcon, contestkorea, detizen, campuspick, allforyoung, dacon, devpost, kaggle) do not list — government, local-government and public-agency notice boards, academic societies, companies' own contests, overseas institutions — via recency web search, verifies each on its official page, writes survivors to a JSON file and registers them with `ach add --from-file` (which runs the duplicate check per item). Never edits the registry directly. ([FMsongX2/Autocontest-Harness](https://github.com/FMsongX2/Autocontest-Harness/blob/HEAD/agents/ContestScout.md))
